- **Exit criteria:** Confirmed by completed T159.1–T159.5: explicit inventory and confirmation with a durable import identity; resumable empty-account import across all five domains with stable operation IDs, interruption/lost-acknowledgement recovery, and second-browser rehydration; deterministic existing-account merge rules with additive trick-state/immutable-record handling, deduplication, and durable same-ID conflicts; explicit profile and per-record conflict decisions that block completion until resolved; and acknowledgement-first, replayable selective cleanup that retains every unacknowledged or rejected guest source record. T156 and T157 dependencies are archived Done, and the full repository verifier passes all four steps (tests, lint, strict typecheck, web export). Native validation is deferred by active web-only project scope.
- **Notes:** Umbrella from S036. Complete T159.1–T159.5. Depends on T157 and the identity isolation established by T156. Child completion evidence remains in this file under T159.1–T159.5; no additional product code is required for umbrella closure.

### [T159.5] Finalize guest import only after acknowledgements

- **Area:** Guest import recovery
- **Priority:** High
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Complete resumability and cleanup so acknowledged source records are tracked durably, retries cannot duplicate server data, and the guest namespace is cleared only after all accepted records and choices are confirmed.
- **Exit criteria:** Fault-injection tests interrupt every import phase, restart with the same import identity, and prove accepted records are not duplicated, rejected/unresolved records remain available, guest data is never cleared early, and successful completion switches to the account namespace with a matching second-browser dataset. Confirmed: durable source-cleanup status and acknowledgement timestamps now survive reload; attempt metadata is persisted before each request; retries reuse operation IDs; cleanup is performed only after acknowledgement and is replayable after cleanup failure; malformed/duplicate progress operations are rejected; focused fault-injection and second-browser suites pass, and the full verifier passes all four steps. Native validation is deferred by active web-only project scope.

### [T160.2] Add per-record conflict resolution

- **Area:** Synchronization conflicts

- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Add `/app/profile/sync-conflicts` with typed comparisons and explicit local/server resolution for profile, custom-trick, goal, and note conflicts, while immutable-session duplicates follow their deterministic identity rule.
- **Exit criteria:** Route/state tests render representative conflicts for every mutable resource, apply either resolution as a new revision-aware operation, persist unresolved conflicts across reload, remove only resolved items, and leave unrelated outbox operations running. Confirmed: added the serializable validated conflict contract and persisted per-envelope records; transport adapters now retain current server rows for comparisons; all four mutable domain drivers record conflicts and guard resolution by identity, generation, record, and operation ID. Keep-local updates the server revision cache and enqueues a fresh operation ID; keep-server validates/adopts typed rows and tombstones while suppressing feedback writes; sibling outbox/conflict records remain untouched. Profile now links to the hydration-gated `/app/profile/sync-conflicts` route, with stable ordering, explicit accessible choices, and no-conflicts state. Focused conflict/envelope/component tests pass, and `node .claude/scripts/verify.mjs --json` passes all four steps (test, lint, typecheck, web export). Native validation is deferred by active project scope; immutable practice-history conflicts remain excluded by deterministic insert-only identity handling.
- **Notes:** From S037. Depends on T160.1 and T157.4–T157.8. No automatic last-write-wins option.

### [T160.1] Add a trustworthy synchronization status model and Profile surface

- **Area:** Profile / sync status
- **Priority:** High
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Derive a serializable user-facing status from coordinator/outbox facts—last successful sync, pending count, syncing, offline, retryable failure, and conflicts—and render it in Profile with a manual retry action.
- **Exit criteria:** Selector and component tests cover every mutually exclusive/combined state, pluralization and timestamps, retry dispatch, reload persistence, and identity changes; the UI never reports "synced" while pending or conflicting operations exist. Confirmed: added the pure serializable sync-status selector with explicit precedence and combined facts, deterministic count/timestamp formatters, durable `lastFailureKind` outbox metadata for retryable failures versus conflicts, and identity-namespaced successful-sync freshness persistence in SyncCoordinatorProvider. Profile now shows syncing, offline, pending, retryable failure, conflict, synced, and no-sync-yet copy with a manual retry action; it never reports synced while pending/conflicting/retryable operations remain. Focused sync/domain/Profile tests pass, and the full verifier passes all four steps (132 suites / 1,684 tests, lint, typecheck, web export). Native validation is deferred by active project scope; conflict-resolution UI remains T160.2.
- **Notes:** From S037. Depends on T157.9. Keep transient runtime handles outside provider/domain state.


### [T159.1] Model resumable guest import and preview its scope

- **Area:** Guest import orchestration
- **Priority:** High
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Add an explicit import state machine and durable progress record that inventories guest/cloud counts by domain before mutation, owns an import identity, and can resume after reload or network loss.
- **Exit criteria:** State-machine tests cover inventory, awaiting confirmation, uploading, awaiting profile choice, conflicts, interruption, resume, completion, cancellation, and identity change; the UI shows accurate per-domain local/cloud counts before any guest record is uploaded. Confirmed: new src/features/guest-import/ feature, mirroring coordinator.ts/logout-guard.ts's pure-reducer-plus-driver-hook split. model/guest-import.ts is a pure state machine (idle/inventorying/awaitingConfirmation/uploading/awaitingProfileChoice/conflicts/completed/cancelled) with no clocks/effects, guarded transitions (stale importId, re-entrant idle, cancel only from awaitingConfirmation), a resumeRequested action (namespace-checked adopt) and a stateRestored action (unconditional hydration adopt), and an identityChanged cancellation mirroring the sync coordinator's own; guest-import.test.ts covers every phase plus the full uploading->awaitingProfileChoice->conflicts->completed and uploading->completed (empty-target) chains, all stale-importId no-ops, resumeRequested match/mismatch, and identityChanged per status. persistence/guest-import-persistence.ts is a version-tagged durable record (mirroring user-profile-persistence.ts) with a full runtime validator and round-trip/malformed-shape/wrong-version tests. persistence/guest-import-inventory.ts is read-only: countLocalGuestRecords reuses the five existing loadXxx functions against a createGuestMigratingStorage-scoped guest storage (proven to pick up the pre-T156.2 legacy bare-key fallback), and countCloudRecords pulls all five existing sync transports in a fixed order with a deterministic first-failing-domain short-circuit (proven via call-log assertions), reusing mapXRowsToDomainCollection's own tombstone exclusion for goals/notes; extractScalarFields was exported from profile-cloud-mapping.ts for reuse rather than re-derived. state/use-guest-import.ts is the driver hook (useIdentityScopedDomain-backed persistence, one auto-inventory attempt per identity generation via an attemptedGenerationRef guard -- found and fixed an infinite-retry loop during testing, since inventoryFailed clears back to idle/no-importId which would otherwise immediately re-trigger the same effect forever) exposing confirmImport/cancelImport/dismiss; use-guest-import.test.tsx (9 cases) proves guest identities never inventory, auto-inventory idle->inventorying->awaitingConfirmation with correct targetKind derivation, confirmImport moves to uploading without settling any domain, remount-resume without re-inventorying for a persisted awaitingConfirmation record vs. re-running for a persisted inventorying record, an identity change resetting and re-inventorying independently, and a null-client/cloud-failure landing in idle with errorMessage set instead of hanging. components/guest-import-preview.tsx is presentational (hidden for idle/cancelled/completed/guest/all-zero-local awaitingConfirmation, a loading row for inventorying, per-domain local/cloud counts plus Import/Not now buttons for a populated awaitingConfirmation, and an inert progress row for uploading/awaitingProfileChoice/conflicts), mounted in src/app/app/profile/index.tsx directly after AccountSection; guest-import-preview.test.tsx (10 cases, hook mocked) proves every visibility/count/button-wiring case. Deviation from the plan's illustrative wording: the driver hook wires hydration to `stateRestored` (unconditional adopt per the plan's own step-2 design), so a resumed mid-flight status becomes the live status directly on hydration -- `resumeRequested` stays fully implemented and reducer-tested for a future caller outside this hook's own hydration path, rather than being dispatched a second time redundantly (its own guard requires status 'idle', which a restored mid-flight status already fails). `node .claude/scripts/verify.mjs` (4/4 steps: test/lint/typecheck/web-export) passes clean; a local `yarn expo start --web` smoke check confirmed `/app/profile` bundles and serves with no runtime errors.
- **Notes:** From S036. Depends on T157.9. Keep effects/clocks/network calls outside the reducer. No real upload/merge/conflict-resolution effect exists yet -- deliberately deferred to T159.2 (empty account), T159.3 (existing-account merge), T159.4 (profile choice + conflicts), and T159.5 (finalize/clear).

### [T158] Protect logout when account changes are unsynchronized

- **Area:** Authentication / synchronization boundary
- **Priority:** High
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Upgrade current-device logout to inspect the current identity's durable outboxes and offer Retry, Cancel, or Discard and log out, then clear in-memory account state without exposing or submitting it under the guest namespace.
- **Exit criteria:** Provider/browser tests cover no-pending logout, successful Retry then logout, failed Retry with the prompt retained, Cancel, explicit Discard, offline behavior, and identity changes during the prompt; other-device sessions remain signed in and subsequent guest/user identities cannot access discarded account cache or operations. Confirmed: added a new shared primitive, discardPendingOperations (and an outboxDiscarded action/reducer case), to sync-envelope.ts -- inherited by all five domains sync-envelope reducer aliases with zero per-domain edits. Added saveNow to useIdentityScopedDomain, a thin wrapper around the hooks existing internal enqueueWrite queue, so a discard write goes through the same FIFO queue as every other write for that domain (no second, independent writer that could race a stale queued write). Each of the five use-X-domain-sync.ts hooks gained two new surface fields, pendingCount (a plain render-time envelope.state.outbox.length, deliberately not read through latestRef so it stays reactive) and discardPending() (durably clears that domains outbox via saveNow, then dispatches outboxDiscarded to sync the in-memory copy) -- re-exported one-for-one by each providers XSync interface. SyncCoordinatorProvider aggregates these into pendingCount (sum across all five domains) and discardAllPending() (Promise.all across all five discardPending() calls), the one piece shared with T160.1s eventual scope. New pure reducer logout-guard.ts (idle/prompt/retrying/discarding/signingOut) and its React driver use-logout-guard.ts wire signOut(), useSyncCoordinatorStatus() (state/requestManualSync/pendingCount/discardAllPending), and useIdentityHandle() (generation) together: a no-pending press skips straight to signingOut; a pending press opens the prompt; Retry calls requestManualSync() and waits for one full running->idle transition of the shared coordinator (armedRef/sawRunningRef, verified against both a fresh run and one that coalesces into an already-running run) before reading the live pendingCount; Discard is unconditional; and a generation change observed while any non-idle flow is armed dispatches reset immediately, on top of (not instead of) the structural checkIdentity/generation guards already in discardPendingOperations, the coordinators own identityChanged cancellation, and AccountSections authenticated-only render guard. AccountSection now renders a new logout-prompt.tsx overlay (RankUpCelebrationModal-style absolute-fill View, no RN Modal precedent in this codebase) offering Retry/Discard and log out/Cancel with retry-failed retained copy, replacing the old local isSubmitting/isSubmittingRef double-press guard with the reducers own status-transition guard. Proven by: sync-envelope.test.ts (discard clears/no-ops/rejects-cross-identity/round-trips), use-identity-scoped-domain.test.tsx (saveNow orders against an in-flight effect write and no-ops on a superseded generation), all five use-X-domain-sync.test.tsx suites (pendingCount reactivity, discard clears+persists+no-ops+is identity-scoped), a new sync-coordinator-provider.test.tsx (real five-provider tree proving the pendingCount sum and discardAllPending fan-out), logout-guard.test.ts (every reducer transition), use-logout-guard.test.tsx (10 cases incl. a coalesced-run and a same-tick-fast-domain-sync race), account-section.test.tsx (18 cases, the 9 pre-existing plus 6 new prompt scenarios end-to-end through the real hook), and a new logout-guard.discard-isolation.test.tsx (real five-domain-provider tree, durable AsyncStorage proof that a discarded outbox is empty on disk and neither a fresh guest nor a later same-user generation observes it). scope: 'local' in auth-provider.tsx signOut is unchanged, so other-device sessions remaining signed in needed no new test. node .claude/scripts/verify.mjs (4/4 steps) passes clean.
- **Notes:** From S038. Depends on T154.2, T156.3, and T157.9. Account-deletion cleanup is separate in T161.5. Known limitation (deliberate, documented rather than built around): Discard clears only the sync obligation, not the local edit itself -- an abandoned local-only edit stays in the primary domains persisted state and is not automatically re-queued for push on a later login by this same user (no revert-to-last-synced-snapshot mechanism exists in this codebase; building one is materially larger than this tickets scope). No general sync-status model or Profile-wide surface was added -- pendingCount/discardAllPending on SyncCoordinatorProvider are additive primitives T160.1 is expected to reuse, not a preview of that tickets own UI.

### [T170] Make sign-up, sign-in, forgot-password, and reset-password follow the user's theme

- **Area:** Authentication UI
- **Priority:** Medium
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** The four `/auth/*` route-group screens (sign-up, sign-in, forgot-password, reset-password) sit outside `/app/*`, so they only ever see the root `_layout.tsx`'s `DefaultAppThemeProvider` (the fixed prototype palette), never the user's personalized `themeBackgroundColor`/`themeAccentColor` — even though each already calls `<Screen>` and would pick up a real theme automatically if one were available. Give this route group access to the user's resolved theme (guest default when none is set) instead of the fixed provider.
- **Exit criteria:** Component tests for all four screens pass, including one asserting each renders with the user's persisted theme background/accent when a preference exists, and the same default palette as today when none does (guest with no theme set). Confirmed in-browser: a user with a non-default theme sees it reflected on sign-up.
- **Notes:** From S049. `Required` because this is a provider/routing-boundary change (which theme provider the `/auth/*` group mounts under), not a per-screen fix — it resolves the QA comment about sign-up specifically, and is the same root cause behind T174's background half. Must not reintroduce T145's landing-page hydration-flicker fix (the `/auth/*` group's theme source must not gate first paint on persistence hydration the way the pre-T145 `/` route did). Added `readCachedGuestTheme`/`useCachedGuestTheme` (mirroring T145's `read-cached-theme-background.ts`/`use-cached-onboarding-status.ts` synchronous pre-hydration cache-read pattern, gated by the same `useSyncExternalStore` hydration-mismatch guard so there is no blocking fallback screen and no reintroduced hydration flash) and a new `CachedAppThemeProvider`, mounted only in `src/app/auth/_layout.tsx` (root layout and `/app/*` untouched). Deliberately diverges from the two older T145 helpers by reading the identity-scoped guest key (`guest:daily-dama:user-profile`) first, falling back to the bare legacy key — those two older helpers still read only the stale bare key post-T156.2 identity namespacing, an undiscovered gap flagged as a follow-up candidate, not fixed here. `reset-password` reads the same guest-scoped key as a documented best-effort approximation (it renders a real `user:<uuid>` recovery session, not a guest one) rather than reintroducing `IdentityProvider`'s hydration gate. Deviation from the plan: the plan assumed all four screens wrap their entire render in `<Screen>`; in the actual code, `<Screen>` only wraps the initializing/redirecting/placeholder branches — the primary form (guest/passwordRecovery) renders `FormScroll` directly. Verified theme propagation instead through `Button`'s `theme.buttonBackground`/`theme.accent` (read directly from `ThemeContext` regardless of `Screen`), and confirmed the true page background is a separate, out-of-scope concern (`+html.tsx`'s static `html,body` CSS, the other half of T174/T145's original fix). Verified in-browser with a local Playwright script against a real Expo web dev server: seeding `guest:daily-dama:user-profile` in `localStorage` with custom `themeBackgroundColor`/`themeAccentColor` and loading `/auth/sign-up`, `/auth/sign-in`, `/auth/forgot-password`, and `/auth/reset-password` all reflected the custom accent/derived button colors; clearing storage reflected the exact default palette; no hydration-mismatch console warnings or errors appeared. `node .claude/scripts/verify.mjs` (full sequence including web export) passes.

### [T168] Make the whole trick card tappable when picking a trick for a new goal

- **Area:** Tricks / Goals
- **Priority:** Medium
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** In the trick picker used when creating a new goal (`trick-directory.tsx`), the "Choose" action is a small `RowAction` Pressable separate from the row's identity content. Apply T143's whole-card-tappable pattern here: tapping anywhere on the row selects the trick, matching the same visible press feedback T143 established for `GoalCard`.
- **Exit criteria:** `yarn test --runInBand` passes, including a test asserting a row-level press in the goal-creation trick picker invokes the same selection behavior as today's "Choose" button, and that the row's other nested interactive controls (edit/delete/notes/goals/practice actions, depending on context) remain independently functional and don't also trigger selection. Confirmed: `TrickRow` now branches its outer container on `onActivate` — when set, the whole row (badge + name + meta) renders as a new `styled(Pressable)` (`ActivatableTrickRowContainer`), sharing `TrickRowContainer`'s layout/CSS via a common `trickRowLayout` block, dimming to 70% opacity while pressed (matching T143's `GoalCardPressable`) and carrying the same `Choose ${trick.name}` accessibility label/`onPress` the old small `RowAction` had. The nested Edit/Delete/Notes/Goals `RowAction` handlers now call `event?.stopPropagation?.()` before running their own callback (defensive on web; `react-test-renderer` doesn't simulate real bubbling). The `Link`-wrapped Practice action is left as a documented, accepted gap since `practiceAction` and `onActivate` never coexist at any current call site. `trick-directory.test.tsx` gained two tests: one asserting a row-level press on the goal-picker's "Choose" row invokes `onActivate` with the right trick and that the pressed node's own subtree contains the trick's name text; one asserting Edit/Delete/Notes/Goals each fire their own callback independently and never also trigger `onActivate`, while pressing the row itself still does. `yarn test --runInBand` (119 suites/1502 tests), full `yarn tsc --noEmit`, and `yarn eslint` on changed files all pass clean; also reran the onboarding, multi-trick Sesh entry, custom-trick-form, all-trick-goals-screen, and all-goals-route suites as a regression check on `TrickRowContainer`'s unchanged-when-no-`onActivate` behavior.
- **Notes:** From S047. `Required` because `trick-directory.tsx`'s row is reused across several contexts with different, mutually exclusive nested interactive children — unlike `GoalRow`'s narrower reuse, wrapping the whole row in one outer `Pressable` needs care that other call sites' existing nested presses (stop-propagation) keep working.

### [T165] Preselect the Default theme preset and remove "Skip for now" from onboarding

- **Area:** Onboarding
- **Priority:** Medium
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Implementer
- **Description:** `theme-presets.ts` has a `DEFAULT_THEME_PRESET_ID` sentinel used only by the Profile screen's preset grid (to clear the color preference), but no actual "Default" entry in `RAINBOW_THEME_PRESETS`, and `OnboardingThemeStep` still renders a "Skip for now" button (`onSkip`) wired identically to `onFinish`. Add a "Default" preset card, pre-selected on entering the onboarding theme step, and remove the "Skip for now" control entirely.
- **Exit criteria:** `yarn test --runInBand` passes, including a test asserting the onboarding theme step shows a "Default" preset pre-selected on mount, no "Skip for now" control renders, and tapping "Finish onboarding" with no other selection completes onboarding on the default theme. Confirmed: added `DEFAULT_THEME_PRESET` (an actual `ThemePreset` object, reusing `DEFAULT_THEME_PRESET_ID`) to `theme-presets.ts`, and had `RainbowThemePresets` (Profile) consume the shared export instead of defining its own local copy. `OnboardingThemeStep` now renders `[DEFAULT_THEME_PRESET, ...RAINBOW_THEME_PRESETS]` and dropped the `onSkip` prop/button entirely. `kentei-onboarding.tsx`'s `themeSelect` case now derives `selectedPresetId` as the Default id whenever both theme colors are `undefined` (the initial-entry state), and its `onSelectPreset` special-cases the Default id to clear colors via `setThemeColors(undefined, undefined)`, mirroring the Profile screen's existing special-case handling. - **Notes:** From S044, extends T146. `onSkip` previously duplicated `onFinish`'s call exactly — nothing else depended on a distinct skip path. ### [T157.9] Trigger synchronization without disrupting active practice

- **Area:** Synchronization lifecycle
- **Priority:** High
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Wire login, foreground, local mutation, reconnection, and manual-retry triggers into the coordinator with debouncing/coalescing appropriate to each event and an explicit guard against synchronization effects changing Sesh state.
- **Exit criteria:** Integration tests fire overlapping login/foreground/reconnect/local-mutation/manual events and prove they coalesce into single-flight runs, offline events remain pending, foreground during every active Sesh status leaves the Sesh state machine unchanged, and unmount/logout cancels subscriptions and stale work. Confirmed: added a new pure-React driver hook, `use-sync-coordinator`.ts, wrapping the already-fully-tested syncCoordinatorReducer (T157.2) -- deliberately new files, not a rewrite of the reducer, per that files own "a future driver" header comment. Every domain-local sync hook (use-profile-domain-sync.ts, use-custom-trick-domain-sync.ts, use-practice-history-domain-sync.ts, use-goal-domain-sync.ts, use-note-domain-sync.ts) gained two new surface fields, hasHydrated/localMutationTick, and had its own ad hoc per-domain login-trigger effect deleted -- login triggering is now owned exclusively by the coordinator, avoiding a double-trigger per domain. `use-sync-coordinator`.ts wires: login (fires once per identity generation once every domain reports hydrated), foreground (AppState active, throttled by FOREGROUND_MIN_INTERVAL_MS = 15s, mirroring use-live-clock.tss own convention), local mutation (debounced by LOCAL_MUTATION_DEBOUNCE_MS = 800ms via a real setTimeout in the driver, not the reducer), reconnect (a plain web-only window.addEventListener('online', ...) signal, explicitly not a Realtime subscription), and manual retry (requestManualSync) -- all funneled through one requestSync(trigger) into the shared reducer, whose existing queuedRerunTrigger coalescing satisfies single-flight coalescing with no additional debounce needed beyond the two above. Identity-change (logout/account-switch) cancels any in-flight run and clears a pending local-mutation debounce timer synchronously; an isMountedRef guard plus the reducers own runId/generation checks make unmount and stale-identity results inert. Added sync-coordinator-provider.tsx, a thin context wrapper mounted innermost in src/app/app/_layout.tsx (inside all five domain providers, since it calls all five useXSync() hooks, each of which throws outside its own provider) exposing useSyncCoordinatorStatus() for a future T160.1 surface; no UI consumes it yet. Proven by two new suites: `use-sync-coordinator`.test.tsx (10 cases with injected fake domain handles: a single trigger completing across all five domains; two overlapping manual triggers coalescing into one in-flight run with the coalesced trigger automatically starting a second run once the first settles; all five domains failing/offline still reaching 'completed' without ever wedging, then a later trigger succeeding normally; login firing exactly once per generation once every domain hydrates; local-mutation debounce collapsing three rapid ticks into one run; foreground throttling two rapid events into one run then allowing a third after the interval; a simulated browser online event; a manual retry; unmount clearing a pending debounce timer with no console errors; and an identity change mid-run immediately reflecting idle at the new generation with the stale in-flight results proven inert) and sync-coordinator.sesh-isolation.test.tsx (the fully-wired proof: mounts all five real domain providers, the real SyncCoordinatorProvider, and the real Sesh component with fake transports seeded with data meaningfully different from local defaults, then asserts Seshs rendered output is byte-identical immediately before/after sync activity through every one of choosing/countdown/active/completed, plus a fully-wired coalescing proof via a call-count spy on the profile transports pull showing two overlapping triggers -- a manual retry immediately followed by a simulated reconnect -- start only one run before settling, then automatically start exactly one more). Deviation from the plans own pre-flight grep: the plan asserted no existing domain-hook test relied on the now-deleted ad hoc login effect, but seven "second-browser rehydrate"-style tests across all five domain-hook suites turned out to genuinely depend on it firing the pull-adopt automatically on mount; fixed by making each of those tests explicitly drive captured.runSync?.('manual'), matching every other assertions already-explicit convention in those files. node .claude/scripts/verify.mjs (4/4 steps) passes clean.
- **Notes:** From S035. Depends on T157.4-T157.8. Do not add Realtime subscriptions.

### [T154.1] Add signed-in password and email change flows

- **Area:** Account security UI
- **Priority:** Medium
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Configure Supabase secure email change and add `/app/profile/account/security` with current-password verification for password changes, including reauthentication, expiry, generic errors, and confirmation guidance through T153.5's shared boundary.
- **Exit criteria:** Route/provider tests and a browser walkthrough confirm a signed-in user can change a password only after supplying the current password, can initiate and complete secure email change, and receives actionable expired/offline states without exposing sensitive values in URLs, logs, or analytics. Confirmed: `AuthProvider.changePassword` (`src/features/auth/state/auth-provider.tsx`) verifies the current password via a real `signInWithPassword` re-login against the account's own known email before calling `updateUser({ password })` -- app-layer reauthentication instead of GoTrue's `reauthenticate()`/`secure_password_change` (left `false`, now documented in `supabase/config.toml`). `AuthProvider.changeEmail` calls `updateUser({ email }, { emailRedirectTo })` with a fixed redirect carrying a `flow=email_change` discriminator. Live-probed against local GoTrue (`double_confirm_changes = true`, unchanged from T153.1) that neither `redirectType` nor the completing session's `new_email` field reliably distinguishes an email-change confirmation from a signup confirmation -- `new_email` is only present on the *first*-clicked of the two required confirmation links (which carries no exchangeable code at all), and is already cleared by the time the *second*-clicked link's code is exchanged -- so `AuthCallbackScreen` was extended with an app-chosen `flow` query param instead (verified live that GoTrue's redirect-URL allowlist match ignores the query string, so no `additional_redirect_urls`/`uri_allow_list` change was needed locally). Also added handling for GoTrue's own `message`-only mid-flow redirect (the first of the two confirmation clicks): shows "One more step." guidance rather than the generic expired-link error. New `AccountSecurityScreen` (`src/features/auth/components/account-security-screen.tsx`) at route `/app/profile/account/security` (`src/app/app/profile/account/security.tsx`, gated by `useRequireOnboarding` matching `kentei-assessment.tsx`'s precedent) renders two independent sections (password, email), each with its own submit/error/success state, a same-tick double-submit guard, and fixed, closed-set error copy (`invalidCredentials` renders a specific "Your current password is incorrect." string here only, since the caller is already authenticated and this is not an enumeration risk). `AccountSection` (Profile) gained a "Manage account security" link. Test coverage: `auth-provider.test.tsx` (`changePassword`/`changeEmail` success, wrong-current-password, offline on either step, not-authenticated guard), `auth-callback-screen.test.tsx` (`flow=email_change` routes to the security route; unrecognized/missing `flow` falls back closed to `/app/home`; a `message`-only redirect shows mid-flow guidance, not an error), `account-security-screen.test.tsx` (every screen-state/validation/error/success/double-submit/no-raw-password-rendered case), `account-section.test.tsx` (link present only when authenticated). New Playwright suite `e2e/auth/07-account-security.spec.ts` (run via `yarn test:e2e:auth`, all 33 tests across `e2e/auth/00`-`07` passed, `33 passed (2.0m)`): wrong-then-correct current password with a real sign-out/sign-in round trip proving the new password took effect and the old one no longer works; offline password/email change; invalid new-email shape; a full real two-address `double_confirm_changes` email-change confirmation (both Mailpit links) landing on `/app/profile/account/security` and reflecting the new email without a manual reload via the existing `onAuthStateChange` -> `USER_UPDATED` path; a stale replay of the completed link rejected; redaction checks confirming no raw password or new email ever reaches the console, Vercel Analytics, or the URL. Discovered and fixed a real e2e-infra gap while building this: `seedConfirmedUser` (`e2e/auth/support/local-supabase.ts`) now returns the created user's id, since a signed-in test's local profile document must be seeded at the identity-namespaced storage key (`user:<uuid>:daily-dama:user-profile`, per `scoped-storage.ts`), not the bare/legacy guest-only key `00-guest-route-access.spec.ts` uses. `node .claude/scripts/verify.mjs` passed all four steps (test, lint, typecheck, web export).
- **Notes:** From S033 and roadmap Phase 1 backend item 5/frontend item 5. Depends on T153.2, T153.3, T153.4, T153.5, and T153.7. T153.7 establishes the callback allowlist; this task adds only the fixed secure-email-change result needed by this route. Do not add MFA or account deletion. Hosted-project verification (confirming/setting the hosted equivalent of `double_confirm_changes`, e.g. `mailer_secure_email_change_enabled`, and confirming the hosted `secure_password_change` equivalent stays off) was deferred -- this run's environment had no Management API personal access token for the hosted `Daily Dama Dev` project. Recorded as an open item in `supabase/HOSTED_DEV_CONFIG.md`'s 2026-08-24 update; whoever next has that access should confirm and close it. All other exit-criteria evidence is local-stack-verified per above.

### [T161.1] Implement the authenticated delete-account Edge Function

- **Area:** Supabase Edge Function
- **Priority:** High
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Add `POST /functions/v1/delete-account` in plain JavaScript with JSDoc and runtime request validation; require a valid JWT, fetch trusted identity server-side, validate `currentPassword` plus exact `DELETE`, and use the service-role client only inside Supabase secrets to remove the Auth user.
- **Exit criteria:** Local function tests prove valid input returns `204` and cascades all application rows, while missing/invalid JWTs, malformed bodies, wrong confirmation, wrong passwords, and attempts to supply another identity are rejected; the service-role key is absent from app environment values and web bundles. Confirmed: added `supabase/functions/delete-account/index.js` (plain JS + JSDoc, `verify_jwt = true` in `supabase/config.toml`) and `supabase/tests/functions/t161_1_delete_account.test.mjs` (`yarn test:functions`, folded into `yarn test:db`). Against a local reset stack, all 16 cases pass: valid deletion returns `204`/`Cache-Control: no-store`/empty body and cascades all six exposed tables plus the Auth user; missing/invalid JWT, seven malformed-body shapes, four wrong-confirmation variants (including case and trailing-space), wrong password, and a body with an extra `userId` naming another user are all rejected (`401`/`400`) while leaving the target user and rows intact. `grep` confirms no `SERVICE_ROLE` reference in `.env.example`/`app.json`/`src`, and the static web export contains no `SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` string. `node .claude/scripts/verify.mjs` (test/lint/typecheck/web-export) passes.
- **Notes:** From S039 and the roadmap Edge Function contract. Depends on T155.7 and T153.5. Deviation from plan: the local Supabase CLI requires an explicit `entrypoint = "./functions/delete-account/index.js"` in `[functions.delete-account]` to serve a plain-`.js` function (its default lookup is `index.ts`), not called out in the plan. Cascade/row-intact assertions in the test suite use the target user's own (pre-deletion) access token against PostgREST rather than the service-role key: this schema's per-table PostgREST grants are scoped to `authenticated` only, so `service_role` (RLS-bypassing but ungranted at the table level) gets `42501` there; PostgREST verifies JWTs by signature alone and never checks `auth.users` existence, so the pre-deletion token still correctly proves zero rows post-cascade. Auth-user-existence checks (`GET /auth/v1/admin/users/<id>`) still use the service-role key against GoTrue's admin API, unaffected by this.

Re-ran fresh against a clean worktree: `yarn test:e2e:auth` reset the local database and passed all 26 tests (`26 passed (1.4m)`), covering guest access to all five `/app` top-level routes while Auth is initializing/offline/unavailable (`00`), sign-in equivalence for wrong-password vs. unknown-email (`01`), missing/malformed/reused/verifier-mismatched callbacks (`02`), open-redirect rejection on sign-up and forgot-password (`03`), the full real-email signup/verification/recovery/reset/resend-limit sequence (`04`), the expired/invalid reset-password link guard (`05`), and console/Analytics redaction across wrong-password sign-in, offline signup, callback error, and forgot-password submission (`06`). `node .claude/scripts/verify.mjs` (not `--skip-export`) passed all four steps (test, lint, typecheck, web export); a manual `yarn expo export --platform web` confirmed all six `/auth/*` routes (`sign-in`, `sign-up`, `check-email`, `forgot-password`, `reset-password`, `callback`) are present in the static export output.
