### [T224] Hold the post-verification callback screen visible briefly before entering Home

- **Area:** Authentication
- **Priority:** Low
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Implementer
- **Description:** `auth-callback-screen.tsx`'s default-branch success path (signup email verification) calls `router.replace({ pathname: '/app/home', params: { newAccount: '1' } })` immediately once `exchangeCodeForSession` resolves, which can happen fast enough that the transitional screen flashes. Add a minimum-visible-duration floor (a fixed constant, in the style of `sesh.tsx`'s existing `CELEBRATION_VISIBLE_MS`) before that specific `router.replace` call only, measured from when the effect starts — never adding extra delay on top of an already-slow exchange.
- **Exit criteria:** `auth-callback-screen.test.tsx` passes with a case (fake timers) asserting the default-branch redirect does not fire before the minimum duration elapses even when `exchangeCodeForSession` resolves instantly, and does fire once it does. The error, `recovery`, and `email_change` branches' existing redirect-timing tests still pass unmodified. Confirmed in-browser that a fast signup-verification round trip still shows the callback screen briefly before landing on Home. Met: added `CALLBACK_MIN_VISIBLE_MS` (600ms) constant gating only the default-branch `router.replace` call, measured from effect start via a captured timestamp, with the pending timer cleared on unmount. `yarn test --runInBand --runTestsByPath src/features/auth/components/__tests__/auth-callback-screen.test.tsx` passes, including new cases asserting the redirect does not fire before the floor and does fire once it elapses; the recovery/email_change/error branch tests are unmodified and still pass. Full `yarn tsc --noEmit` and `yarn lint` pass. Browser-verified against a local Expo web dev server plus the local Supabase Auth CLI stack: a real signup-confirmation link round trip showed "Finishing sign-in..." before transitioning to Home with the "Your account is verified and you're signed in." banner.
- **Notes:** From S104. Deliberately scoped to the default branch only — the routine sign-in redirect (`sign-in-screen.tsx`) and the callback screen's other branches (error, recovery, email-change) are unaffected and shouldn't gain an artificial delay without reason.

### [T208] Replace Home's new-account banner with a global, celebration-style acknowledgment

- **Area:** Home / Authentication
- **Priority:** Medium
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Required
- **Description:** Replace `home.tsx`'s locally-scoped `NewAccountBanner` (added by T207) with a full-screen, auto-dismissing acknowledgment mounted at the root layout (`src/app/_layout.tsx`), reusing `RankUpCelebrationModal`'s visual pattern (theme-derived full-bleed overlay, `OnboardingRankRevealStep`-style layout) with its own "welcome/verified" copy — not the trick-lace celebration copy or component instance. `auth-callback-screen.tsx`'s existing `newAccount=1` redirect param stays the trigger; since no route-scoped `useLocalSearchParams` read is available above `<Slot />`, the root layout reads it via `useGlobalSearchParams` and strips it the same way `home.tsx` does today.
- **Exit criteria:** A new test at the root-layout level (or an equivalent integration test) asserts the acknowledgment overlay renders once when `newAccount=1` is present anywhere in the URL and never on a routine sign-in redirect; `home.test.tsx`'s now-removed `NewAccountBanner` cases are deleted rather than left dangling. Confirmed in-browser: completing signup + email verification through the real callback link shows the full-screen overlay regardless of what's rendered under it (Home content or onboarding), it auto-dismisses without requiring a tap, appears exactly once, and a regular sign-in shows nothing. Additionally: the overlay's text remains legible (fixed, theme-independent text color) against at least two different accent-color theme presets, it animates in on appearance and fades out on its own within a few seconds with no tap required, and dismissal never requires a manual page refresh. Met: added `NewAccountWelcomeOverlay` (`new-account-welcome-overlay.tsx`), a full-screen `StyleSheet.absoluteFill` overlay reading `theme.accent` (falling back to `AppColors.yellow`) with fixed `AppColors.ink` text (`OnboardingRankRevealStep`'s precedent), a checkmark ink-circle stage, and `sesh.tsx`'s `CelebrationBanner` fade/timer pattern (`NEW_ACCOUNT_WELCOME_AUTO_DISMISS_MS`, 2600ms visible + 250ms fade) calling `onDismiss` with no tap control. `src/app/_layout.tsx` now reads `newAccount` via `useGlobalSearchParams`, captures it once through a lazy `useState` initializer (avoiding a render-then-pop-in flicker), strips it via a ref-guarded one-shot `router.replace(usePathname())` (generalized from `home.tsx`'s hardcoded `/app/home`, since this now sits above every route), and renders the overlay as a sibling of `AuthProvider` inside `DefaultAppThemeProvider` so it survives regardless of what `<Slot />` renders and isn't tied to auth state. `home.tsx`'s T207 `NewAccountBanner`/`HomeSearchParams`/strip-effect/banner styled-components were all removed, along with `home.test.tsx`'s now-obsolete "HomeScreen new-account acknowledgment banner (T207)" describe block and its `mockSearchParams`/`useLocalSearchParams` mock scaffolding (its `mockRouter`/`useRouter` mock is still used elsewhere in the file). New coverage: `new-account-welcome-overlay.test.tsx` (renders with the `Account verified` accessibility label; calls `onDismiss` once after the auto-dismiss duration and not before; clears its timers on unmount) and `src/app/__tests__/root-layout.test.tsx` (mocking `expo-router`, `AnimatedSplashOverlay`, `AuthProvider`, and `@vercel/analytics/react` wholesale per `app-tabs.web.test.tsx`'s precedent) asserting: no overlay and no `router.replace` call on a plain mount; exactly one overlay render plus a stripped `router.replace` call when `newAccount=1` is present; still renders when `newAccount=1` is present alongside another unrelated global param; and the overlay unmounts on its own past the auto-dismiss duration with no simulated tap. `yarn test --runInBand`, `yarn lint`, `yarn tsc --noEmit`, and `yarn expo export --platform web` all pass. Browser-verified against a local Expo web dev server backed by the local Supabase Auth CLI stack (a confirmed test user created via the admin API, since the local stack's GoTrue redirect is fixed to `localhost:8081`, occupied by another concurrent session): signing in normally showed no overlay; navigating with `newAccount=1` (plus an unrelated param) rendered the overlay exactly once with the full viewport as its bounds, correct copy, and `theme.accent` (`#f4c94d`, i.e. `AppColors.yellow`) as its background, immediately stripped the query param without a manual refresh, and auto-dismissed on its own after the timer elapsed. The "at least two different accent-color theme presets" sub-check could not be exercised through this literal live flow: `useResolvedTheme`'s own documented invariant is that theme fields are always `undefined` for the entire lifetime of onboarding, and this overlay only ever fires pre-onboarding for a just-verified new account (per `auth-callback-screen.tsx`'s comment, this redirect never fires for an existing account), so `theme.accent` always resolves to the same fixed default in production regardless of any preset a user later selects. Verified the underlying legibility guarantee instead via the same WCAG contrast math `contrast.ts` already uses: fixed `AppColors.ink` text against the actual runtime background (`AppColors.yellow`, `#f4c94d`) measures a 8.10:1 contrast ratio, well above the 4.5:1 AA minimum.
- **Notes:** From S090. Direction confirmed with the user 2026-09-02: reuse the presentation *mechanism* (full-screen overlay, theme-derived styling per `RankUpCelebrationModal`'s precedent) and *visual identity*, not the trick-lace copy or component instance. Pick an auto-dismiss duration that doesn't reintroduce T145-class flicker risk. Folded in from S101 (2026-09-03 QA on this exact banner, filed before T208 replaces it): ensure the overlay's text stays legible regardless of the active theme — `OnboardingRankRevealStep`'s existing `RevealTitle`/`AppColors.ink` precedent (fixed dark text over a theme-derived background) already does this and should carry over — and reuse `sesh.tsx`'s existing `CelebrationBanner` fade/scale timer pattern (`CELEBRATION_VISIBLE_MS`/`CELEBRATION_FADE_MS`, its `fadeTimer`/`removeTimer` `setTimeout` pair) for the enter/exit animation rather than inventing a new approach. Addressed here instead of a separate ticket because T208 deletes `NewAccountBanner` outright — a standalone fix targeting that soon-to-be-deleted code would be wasted work.

### [T236] Fix the callback screen's copy and extend its minimum-visible duration

- **Area:** Authentication
- **Priority:** Low
- **Status:** Done
- **Planning:** Implementer
- **Description:** `auth-callback-screen.tsx`'s default-branch transitional screen reads "Finishing sign-in..." (line 215) and holds for `CALLBACK_MIN_VISIBLE_MS = 600` (line 32), added by T224. Change the copy to "Finishing sign up..." and raise the constant to 2000.
- **Exit criteria:** `auth-callback-screen.test.tsx` passes, with its existing minimum-duration-floor test updated to assert against the new 2000ms value and any copy-matching assertion updated to "Finishing sign up...". Confirmed in-browser that a fast signup-verification round trip shows "Finishing sign up..." for at least 2 seconds before landing on Home. Met: `CALLBACK_MIN_VISIBLE_MS` raised to 2000 and the default-branch `<PageTitle>` copy changed to "Finishing sign up...", with the test file's `advanceTimersByTime` calls and the minimum-duration-floor boundary case updated to 1999/2000ms; `yarn test --runInBand --runTestsByPath src/features/auth/components/__tests__/auth-callback-screen.test.tsx` passes. Full `yarn tsc --noEmit` passes; `yarn eslint` on the changed files shows only a pre-existing unrelated `import/first` warning. Browser-verified with a Playwright script driving a real signup through a local Expo web dev server (env-configured against the local Supabase Auth CLI stack) and a real Mailpit-delivered confirmation link: navigating `/auth/callback?code=...` showed "Finishing sign up..." immediately, it was still visible at ~1900ms, and the redirect to `/app/home` landed only after ~3.3s total (past the 2000ms floor, plus network/exchange time).

- **Area:** Security tooling

Refreshed `security/accepted-dependency-advisories.json`'s two stale justifications: `nanoid` (1139427) and `uuid` (1119441) now have reachable upstream patches per live advisory data (`>=3.3.18`, `>=11.1.1`) but the lockfile still resolves the older transitive versions (`3.3.17`, `7.0.3`) — bumping those is a `yarn.lock` change and is out of scope here; flagging as a candidate follow-up rather than fixing in this ticket. No dependency overhaul or new advisory exceptions were made.
